LP SOTA may be robust if initialized not stuck.

Let's analyze LP direction from a given f. It solves max sum(b) subject to f*b <= M. This effectively finds a sequence b that, when averaged with f, doesn't increase f*g at any lag beyond current peak. It maximizes mass, so likely moves mass to places that reduce peak's associated products. It should escape uniform? We found uniform LP gives B=S. So exactly uniform stuck. If f has slight nonuniformity, maybe B>S? Let's test n small. If f has low peak M slightly above? The constraint f*b <= M. Since b can be large where f small? But sum b objective. If f has a small value at some index, b can be large there constrained by other lags. This may increase sum beyond S, creating direction that puts more mass where f is small but does not raise cross-correlation too much. Then update f toward b (normalized). This seems like a "best response" for a bilinear game? Could improve.

Implementation details for LP:
- Let f length n, M = max(conv(f,f)). We require f*b[k] <= M for all k=0..2n-2.
- Variables b[i] >=0. Max sum b.
- If optimum sum B <= S (sum f) + tol, no improvement. Then perturb f.
- If B > S, set g = (S / B) b. Update f = (1 - t) f + t g. t maybe 0.1 or choose line search to reduce score/M.

But after update, peak M may increase due to g*g term. We can line search t: try t=0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05 and compute score/M; choose best. Since score only changes with M and S fixed. We can keep S fixed.

LP matrix construction: For each lag k, constraint sum_{i} f[k-i] b[i] <= M, where f[j] defined if 0<=j<n else 0. This is a Toeplitz-like matrix with row k having entries f[k-i] for i from max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k). We can build sparse.

We can solve LP using scipy.optimize.linprog(c=-ones, A_ub=sparse, b_ub=M*ones, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs'). For n=2048, entries ~n^2=4M. 4M entries in sparse maybe ~100MB? Actually COO/CSR: data 4M*8=32MB, indices 4M*4=16MB (int32) or 8? scipy may use intc 4, plus indptr. ~50MB. ok. For n=4096, 16M entries ~200MB, maybe high. Use n <=2048 or 3072. Could build dense for n<=512.

But we can optimize LP constraints: Since M is max f*f, many constraints. Could use active set? But linprog handles.

Problem: LP optimum may be unbounded? If some row all zeros? For k constraints, each variable b[i] appears in rows i..i+n-1, so bounded. If f has zeros, some rows may have zeros; but likely bounded. If unbounded, linprog returns unbounded. Handle.

Alternative use cvxpy with network simplex? GLOP fast for large LP. But cvxpy overhead maybe. SciPy Highs good.

Need not call evaluate_sequence for each LP; internal compute score. But final best verify.

Optimization function per candidate:
1. Normalize sum to 1, clip nonneg.
2. Compute score via FFT. Track global best.
3. Loop until time or no improvement:
   a. Compute c=fft_conv(a), M=max(c), kmax maybe.
   b. If M < best_M_for_length? update.
   c. Try LP direction:
      - Build constraint matrix A (maybe cache per length and a? A depends on a as row coefficients f. Can't cache easily except structure). We can build A each LP or reuse cursor? Since f changes, row values change. Building O(n^2). For n=1024, maybe okay. For many iterations, costly. We can limit LP calls: e.g., after every 200 gradient steps or when stuck.
   d. Gradient/subgradient steps for speed:
      - Use subgradient of F = 2n max(c) (sum=1). g = 2*reverse part of a at kmax. But as noted uniform stuck. Add smooth logsumexp gradient for better.
      - Perform line search: a_new = a - lr*g (or gradient of score with sum constraint). Project nonneg and renormalize. If improved accept, else decrease lr.
      - Add random perturbations to escape: multiplicative log-noise, block perturb, coordinate random.
   e. If no improvement for many steps, do LP if time.

Let's derive FFT gradient correctly to avoid loop.
We want grad[i] = 2 * sum_{k=0}^{2n-2} w_k * a_{k-i}, where 0≤k-i<n.
Let j = k-i, so k=i+j, j=0..n-1, k within 0..2n-2.
grad[i] = 2 * sum_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{i+j} * a_j, with w_k=0 outside.
This is cross-correlation of a and w: grad/2 = (a ⋆ w)[i]? Define cross-correlation C[i] = sum_j a_j * w_{i+j}. Yes.
We can compute C via FFT: For sequences a length n, w length 2n-1. Let N >= n + len(w) -1 = 3n-2. Use FFT of a (padded N) and w (padded N). The linear correlation C[i] = sum_j a_j w_{i+j}. This is convolve(a, reverse(w))? Let's test.
conv(a, w)[m] = sum_j a_j w_{m-j}. If we set m = i + (2n-2?)? We want w_{i+j}. Let w_rev[r] = w_{L-1-r} with L=2n-1. Then conv(a, w_rev)[m] = sum_j a_j w_rev[m-j] = sum_j a_j w_{L-1-(m-j)} = sum_j a_j w_{L-1-m+j}. Let m = L-1-i. Then = sum_j a_j w_{i+j} = C[i]. Great.
So C = ifft(fft(a,N)*fft(w_rev,N)).real[0:n] with m = L-1-i? The convolution output index m. We need m = L-1-i, which ranges from L-1 down to 0. So C[i]=conv_vec[L-1-i]. Alternatively compute correlation via ifft(conj(fft(a))*fft(w)) easier. Let's verify with small example.
But using conv with reversed w: conv_vec length 3n-2. C_rev? Let's just test mentally with n=2, a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. C[0]=a0 w0 + a1 w1. C[1]=a0 w1 + a1 w2. w_rev=[w2,w1,w0]. conv=[a0w2, a0w1+a1w2, a0w0+a1w1, a1w0]. L-1=2. C[0] at m=2: a0w0+a1w1 ✓; C[1] at m=1: a0w1+a1w2 ✓. So grad[i]=2*conv_vec[L-1-i]. Good. Algorithm for local optimize:
```
def local_optimize(a, rng, deadline, max_outer=...):
    n=len(a); N=fft_len(4*n)
    a = normalize(a)
    best=copy; best_score=score_fast(a)
    lr=0.2
    no_improve=0
    T=20
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if timeout break
       c=conv(a)
       # soft weights
       w=softmax(T*c)
       grad=grad_soft(a,w,N)
       gmax=max(grad); if gmax>0: g=grad/gmax
       # try several lr maybe current
       a_trial = a / (1 + lr*g)
       a_trial = normalize(a_trial)
       s=score_fast(a_trial)
       if s < best_score:
          best=...; no_improve=0; lr=min(1.0, lr*1.05)
       else:
          no_improve+=1; lr=max(0.02, lr*0.8)
          # if stuck, add perturbation
          if no_improve>some:
             a = perturb(best, rng)
             lr=0.2
       a = a_trial if accepted else maybe still use trial? For annealing maybe accept occasionally?
```
Since objective nonconvex, accept occasional worse to escape? Could do simulated annealing: if score < best or random<exp(-(s-best)/T_sa). But need best. Track best separately. Use current a may be worse.

Perturbations: multiplicative lognoise on fraction/all: a * exp(sigma*rng.normal), normalize, clip floor. Block perturb. Also "mass shift": pick subset where gradient high and distribute to low.

LP implementation efficient:
```
def lp_direction(a, M):
    n=len(a)
    # Build A sparse rows k, cols i where 0<=k-i<n
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for k in range(2*n-1):
        start=max(0, k-n+1); stop=min(n-1, k)+1
        for i in range(start, stop):
            rows.append(k); cols.append(i); data.append(a[k-i])
    A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2n-1,n))
    c=-np.ones(n)
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2n-1, M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
```
This loop O(n^2) Python slow for n=1024 (~1M appends) maybe 0.1-0.5s. Can vectorize:
For each i, rows are i..i+n-1, values a[0..n-1]? Actually row k, col i value a[k-i]. For fixed i, values a[0],a[1],...,a[n-1] for k=i..i+n-1. So we can build by columns using arange. But O(n^2) append anyway. Use scipy.sparse.diags? A is super/under? It's a banded matrix with n bands? Row k has up to n nonzero. It's like a Hankel? We can build using np.indices? Memory O(n^2). For n=1024 ok. But repeated many times maybe heavy. Could build dense for n<=512 and sparse for larger. Since LP itself heavy, okay.

Gradient soft via correlation:
```
def gradient_from_weights(a, w, N):
    n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    # If w is length L, reverse
    wr = w[::-1]
    fa = fft(a, N)
    fw = fft(wr, N)
    conv = np.real(ifft(fa * fw))
    # conv indices 0..N-1; linear conv length n+L-1=3n-2
    return 2.0 * conv[L-1 : L-1 + n : -1]? Need order C[i]=conv[L-1-i]. For i=0 -> idx L-1; i=n-1 -> idx0.
    return 2.0 * conv[L-1::-1][:n]
```
Check: conv[L-1::-1] yields indices L-1, L-2, ..., 0. Take first n. Good.
But conv may have tiny negative; grad nonnegative? w and a nonnegative, correlation should nonnegative; real FFT errors clip 0.

For hard peak, can avoid FFT: grad = zeros; k=kmax; kmi = k - arange(n); mask; grad[mask]=2*a[kmi[mask]].

Softmax weights:
```
def soft_weights(c, T):
    xc = T*c
    xc -= xc.max()
    e=np.exp(xc)
    return e/e.sum()
```
If T too high, only peak; if too low, sum over all. T maybe 20. For n large c values ~1/n, T*c ~ T/n. If n=1024, T=20 => weights spread over many lags. Need T scale with n? Objective max of c; c typical 1/n. To distinguish peak from average, need T difference*? If peak ~0.75/n, avg~0.5/n, difference 0.25/n, T*diff ~0.25*T/n. For spread, T maybe 10*n? Wait c values are O(1/n). To make exp(T c) emphasize peak, T should be O(n). If T=20, all c ~0.001, exp small ~1, uniform weights, gradient = 2*(sum w)*shift? That may be too smooth. We want log-sum-exp approximating max: T large enough that weights concentrate. But if T*peak ~10, need T ~10n. For n=256, T=2500. Is that okay numerically? c ~0.004, T*c ~4; exp manageable. Use T = 10*n maybe. Then weights concentrate on top few lags. But if all c similar, still spread. We can adapt: T = max(10.0, 10.0*n / max_bandgap?) Actually c magnitude ~1/n. Set target peak exponent e.g., 20: T = 20 * n (since peak*c? cpeak~1/n => T*cpeak ~20). Could be 20*n. For n=1024 T=20480, exp max 20 okay. Use `T = float(20.0 * n)`. But if sequence nonuniform, cpeak may be >1/n, T*cpeak >20; softmax stable with subtract max, okay. If cpeak much larger, weights very concentrated. Good. Use maybe T = 10*n to not too sharp. We can also use hard gradient occasionally.

Pseudo local_optimize:
```
def local_optimize(a, rng, deadline, max_steps=5000, do_lp=True, lp_every=200):
    n=len(a); size=...; N=...
    a=normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_internal(a)
    lr=0.2; T=5*n; no=0; step=0
    while time<deadline:
       # compute c, M
       c=conv_fft(a,size)
       # if score improved update? best_score maybe
       # weights
       T = min(50*n, T*1.002?)
       if step % 2 == 0: use soft grad else hard?
       w=soft_weights(c,T)
       grad=gradient_from_weights(a,w,N)
       gmax=max(grad); if gmax<=0: break
       g=grad/gmax
       # generate trial lrs
       lrs=[lr, lr*0.5, lr*1.5, 0.05, 0.5]
       best_trial_score=inf; best_trial=None
       for l in lrs:
           if l<=0 or not finite: continue
           trial = a / (1.0 + l*g)
           trial = normalize(trial)
           sc=score_internal(trial)
           if sc<best_trial_score: ...
       # maybe accept best trial if < current_score*(1+accept_margin)
       current_score=score_internal(a)
       if best_trial_score < best_score:
          best=best_trial; best_score=best_trial_score; no=0
       if best_trial_score < current_score or rng.random() < 0.1: a=best_trial; lr adjust
       else: no+=1; lr=max(0.01, lr*0.8); if no>50: perturb(a)
       step++
       if do_lp and step % lp_every ==0 and time remains:
           # LP from best? Use current best to avoid wasting? direction from best
           res = lp_direction(best) # pass M
           if res: line search h=(1-t)best + t*(res/sumres)
           update best and a
```
Calling score_internal many times (5 lrs) per step. For n=512, 5*FFT maybe 0.005s? Good. But max_steps limited by time. Maybe use 3 lrs.

Alternatively, do one step and if better increase lr else decrease; add random. Simpler and faster. Use 2 trials.

- smooth noise: add random Fourier low freq to log a, then exp.
- block scaling: multiply random contiguous block by exp(normal).
- coordinate mass shift: pick high values and low values, transfer.

Can also use LP on a relaxed M if no direction. Let's implement `try_lp_update(a, M, rng, deadline, relax_attempts=3)`:
```
def try_lp_update(a, M):
   for factor in [1.0,1.05,1.1,1.2,1.5]:
      res=linprog(factor*M)
      if success:
         b=res.x; B=sum(b)
         if B > 1 + 1e-6:
             g=b/B
             # line search t
             for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,1.0]:
                trial=(1-t)*a + t*g; normalize; score
             return best trial, score
   return None
```
If factor 1 no B>1 but factor >1 gives direction, may increase M but if line search improves score overall, good.

Basis q formula using y=2x-1:
```
def basis_q(n, c, d):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   y=2*x-1
   p=1.0 + c*(3*y*y - 1.0) + d*y*(1-y*y)
   return np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
```

Add random perturbations:
```
def perturb(a, rng, sigma):
   # multiplicative on all or random block
   loga = np.log(a + 1e-12)
   if rng.random()<0.5: loga += sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)
   else: block... loga[start:stop] += sigma*...
   b=np.exp(loga)
   b=np.where(b>1e-12,b,0)
   return normalize(b)
```
But if a zeros, log issues; use a+1e-12.

Pseudo:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s-20)
  best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
  # helper update global: verify with internal, maybe actual every some seconds
  # Step 0: parent? we can get parent but not necessary. Use if exists for inits.
  parent = get_parent()
  # Step 1: param Fourier optimization for continuous shapes at n=64/128
  if time<deadline:
      for n_param in [64,128]:
          for start in range(num_starts):
             x0=...
             res=minimize(objective_param, x0, method='Nelder-Mead', options...)
             seq=param_sequence(x0,n_param)
             score=score_internal(seq)
             update
             if time > param_budget: break
  # Step 2: generate initial sequences list for lengths; include param best, block, random, parent, basis, etc.
  lengths=[16,32,64,128,256,512]
  # We already have best_seq from param; add to best per length.
  # For each length, for each init, do local optimize with chunk time (maybe 20s) using local_optimize with do_lp=False (or True for 256+).
  # But if sequence length 512, many inits could be heavy. We'll allocate.
  for n in lengths:
     inits=make_inits(n, rng, parent, best_seq)
     # limit inits count
     for init in inits:
        if time>deadline: break
        end=time+15
        seq,score=local_optimize(init, rng, deadline=end, do_lp=(n<=256), max_steps=...)
        update
  # Step 3: If time remains, take global best, maybe try resampled to nearby lengths? Actually best length fixed. Run local+LP on best until deadline.
  if best_seq is not None and time<deadline:
      seq=best_seq.copy()
      # maybe if best_score > target, also resample to best length*2? Better stay.
      seq,score=local_optimize(seq, rng, deadline=deadline, do_lp=True, max_steps=...)
      update
  # Final fallback if no best: uniform 64.
  # Verify final with evaluate_sequence: score_actual = evaluate_sequence(best_seq.tolist())
  # If inf, sanitize: clip, set sum.
  # Return list
```
Need ensure param optimization doesn't consume too much. Set param_budget = min(90s, 0.1*budget). For 1500, 90s. Local search remaining 1300.

But local_optimize with LF may be slow. We can adapt: For initial chunks, use gradient only (fast) to improve. Then final uses LP.

Implement `local_optimize(a, rng, deadline, do_lp=False, lp_interval=100, max_steps=None)`:
- Input array normalized; if not, normalize and return if invalid.
- `Nsmall=next_pow2(2*n)`, `Nbig=next_pow2(4*n)`.
- `T = 10*n` initially, anneal to 50*n.
- `current_score = score_internal(a, c, conv_size)`.
- `best_score = current_score; best = a.copy()`.
- `lr_alpha = 1.0` (multiplicative of mean step). In step: g_norm, step_values = alpha * a.mean() * g (if g normalized max=1). Trial alphas = [alpha, alpha*0.3, alpha*3, 0.2, 0.8]. For each, trial = a - alpha*mean_a*g; clip; normalize; if sum positive compute score. Choose best among trials if < current_score + small? We can accept best trial if < best_score or < current_score. To avoid too many FFTs, use 2 alphas: current alpha and 0.5 alpha. But 3 okay.
- After accepting a trial, update lr_alpha: if new_score < current_score: alpha=min(5, alpha*1.1); else alpha=max(0.1, alpha*0.7).
- If no improvement for 50 steps: perturb best or current; alpha=1.0.
- If do_lp and step % 100 ==0 and time > deadline-30: call try_lp on `best` (or current best). If improves, best and current? Set a=best.copy(), current_score=best_score.
- To avoid LP too often, maybe lp_interval=300.

Scoring many trials: For n=512, score_internal FFT size 1024, ~0.001s. 3 trials*1000 steps =3000 FFT ~3s, okay. LP building 512 (262k entries) + solve maybe 0.2-1s every 100 steps (10 times) okay.

But local_optimize called many times with 15s chunks, overhead of LP maybe. Use do_lp False in first chunks.

`try_lp_update(a, c, M, deadline)`:
- if n > 2048: return None.
- Build A vectorized. If M <=0 return.
- Try factor list [1.0, 1.2, 1.5] maybe; break on success with B>1.
- Use `linprog(-one, A_ub=A, b_ub=fbub*ones, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': max(1, deadline-time-5)})`. Linprog options may not accept time_limit? Highs has 'time_limit'. Wrap try.
- If success: b=res.x; B=sum(b); if B <=1*(1+1e-5) continue.
- g=b/B; line t list [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]. trial=(1-t)*a + t*g; if trial negative small clip; normalize; score. Choose best.
- Return (best_trial, score).
- If no, return None.

Note: For a with sum1, M=max c maybe ~0.1/n. If factor 1 B maybe 1. For factor 1.5, constraints looser, may give direction. Use line search to ensure score improves.

`make_inits(n, rng, parent, best_seq)`:
Need arrays length n sum1.
- If best_seq not None: `resample_sequence(best_seq, n)` using np.interp over [0,1]. Add small noise.
- Parent similarly if not None.

def local_optimize(a, rng, deadline, do_lp=False, lp_interval=200):
    n=len(a)

Perturb function:

def perturb_sequence(seq, rng, sigma):
  n=len(seq); a=seq.clip(1e-12)
  loga=np.log(a)
  mode=rng.integers(3)
  if mode==0: loga += sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)
  elif mode==1:
      width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//10), max(3,n//2))); start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width)); loga[start:start+width]+=sigma*rng.standard_normal(width)
  else:
      # move mass: pick high and low indices
      idx=np.argsort(a)[::-1]; ... too complex
  b=np.exp(loga)
  b=np.where(b>1e-12,b,0)
  return normalize(b)
```
Sigma relative? log perturbation; sigma 0.1-1.

`get_parent_sequence()`:
```
def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        arr = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
        if arr is None: return None
        arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float)
        if arr.ndim!=1 or arr.size==0: return None
        arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return None
        s=arr.sum()
        if s<1e-12: return None
        return arr/s
    except: return None
```

`resample_sequence(seq, n)`:
```
def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,float); seq=np.clip(seq,0,None); s=seq.sum(); if s<... return ones(n)/n
    old=np.linspace(0,1,seq.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,n); val=np.interp(new,old,seq)
    return normalize(val)
```

def param_raw_sequence(theta, n):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    y=2*x-1
    A=float(theta[0]); B=float(theta[1]); C=float(theta[2]); D=float(theta[3]); E=float(theta[4]) if len>4 else 0
    p=1.0 + A*(4*x*(1-x)-1.0) + B*np.sin(2*pi*x) + C*(y*(1-y*y)) + D*np.sin(4*pi*x) + E*(3*y*y-1.0)
    return p
```

Let's write `local_optimize` with performance in mind:
```
def local_optimize(seq, rng, deadline, do_lp=False, lp_interval=150):
    n=seq.size
    a=normalize(seq,n)
    c_size=next_pow2(2*n); g_size=next_pow2(4*n)
    c=fft_convolution(a,c_size)
    cur=2.0*n*np.max(c)
    best=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=cur
    alpha=1.0; T=max(10.0, 20.0*n); no=0; step=0; last_lp=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # If multiple LP not, maybe update cur from a? We compute c at end of previous.
        # Ensure cur is score of a (we set after accept). If perturbation changed, compute.
        # At top, if cur from a maybe okay.
        # We need c for gradient; if a changed since c? We maintain.
        # To be safe, compute c if not flag? Simpler compute every loop (cost okay).
        c=fft_convolution(a,c_size)
        cur=2*n*max(c)
        if cur < best_score: best... no=0
        w=soft_weights(c,T)
        grad=gradient_from_weights(a,w,g_size)
        gmax=grad.max()
        if not finite or gmax <= 0: break
        g=grad/gmax
        mean=a.mean()
        best_trial_score=cur; best_trial=a; best_trial_c=c
        for al in (alpha, 0.4*alpha, 2.0*alpha):
            if al<=0: continue
            trial=a - al*mean*g
            trial=normalize(trial,n)
            if not np.any(trial>0): continue
            tc=fft_convolution(trial,c_size)
            ts=2*n*max(tc)
            if ts < best_trial_score:
                 ...
        if best_trial_score < cur:
            a=best_trial; c=best_trial_c; cur=best_trial_score; alpha=min(6.0,alpha*1.15); no=0
        else:
            no+=1; alpha=max(0.05,alpha*0.85)
            if no > 40:
                sigma=[0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]
                a=perturb_sequence(best, rng, float(rng.choice(sigma)))
                c=fft...; cur=...
                alpha=1.0; no=0
        T = min(80.0*n, T*1.01)
        step +=1
        if do_lp and step - last_lp >= lp_interval and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
            # use best not a
            M=best_score/(2.0*n)
            lp_res=try_lp_update(best, M, deadline)
            if lp_res is not None:
                new_best,new_score=lp_res
                if new_score < best_score:
                    best=new_best; best_score=new_score; best_c=fft_convolution(best,c_size)
                    a=best.copy(); c=best_c; cur=best_score; no=0; alpha=1.0
            last_lp=step
    # return best (or maybe a if better) ensure best_score with a? At end check a
    c=fft_convolution(a,c_size); cur=...
    if cur<best_score: best=a; best_score=cur
    return best.copy(), float(best_score)
```
Computing `c=fft_convolution(a,c_size)` every loop duplicates if we already did after update. But simple.

Let's refine param formula to be more powerful. Use 5 parameters:
`p = 1 + A*f_ep + B*s2 + C*f_odd3 + D*s4 + E*f_p2`
where:
- f_ep = 4x(1-x) - 1 (endpoints -1, center +1) times A positive lowers endpoints.
- s2 = sin(2πx), odd about .5.
- f_odd3 = y(1-y^2) (odd, zero endpoints) (same sign as s2? maybe different shape)
- s4 = sin(4πx) (odd about .5? sin(4π(1-x))=-sin4πx yes)
- f_p2 = 3y^2 -1 (even Legendre, endpoints 2, center -1) times E (maybe negative to lower endpoints? But f_ep enough)
This gives flexibility. Param ranges: A,E ∈[-0.5,0.8], others [-1.5,1.5]. Use penalty if p<0.01 maybe. Use Nelder-Mead. Initial starts include our A=0.2,B=0.7,C=0,D=0,E=0 and variations.
Need ensure p roughly sum1? We normalize after, mean not 1 initially; multiplier with A maybe small.
Penalty: `pen = max(0, 1e-3 - min(p))**2 * 1e8`. Also maybe cap p to 10? Not needed.

Let's code:
```
def smooth_random_init(n, rng, K=4):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    p=np.ones(n)
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.5)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*pi)
       p += amp*np.sin(2*pi*m*x + phase)
    return normalize(p,n)
```
```
def basis_q_init(n,c,d):
    x=...; y=...
    p=1 + c*(3*y*y-1) + d*y*(1-y*y)
    return normalize(np.maximum(p,1e-3),n)
```
```
def block_init(weights,n):
    K=len(weights); weights=np.asarray(weights,float); weights=np.maximum(weights,0); s=weights.sum(); if s<=0: return ones
    weights=weights/s
    sizes=np.full(K,n//K); rem=n%K; sizes[:rem]+=1
    out=np.empty(n); idx=0
    for w,sz in zip(weights,sizes):
       if sz>0: out[idx:idx+sz]=w/float(sz)
       idx+=sz
    return normalize(out,n)
```
Pattern list: For K=4 patterns:
- optimal two-block in 4? [1/3,0,2/3,0]? and rotations.
- [0.38,0.12,0.38,0.12] and rotate by 1,2,3; mirror.
- [0.3,0.1,0.3,0.3]? sum1? normalize.
- [0.45,0.05,0.25,0.25] etc.
For K=3: [0.5,0.25,0.25] and rotations, [0.4,0.4,0.2].
For K=5: [0.3,0.15,0.3,0.15,0.1] etc.
But local search will adjust; not need many.

`make_inits` code:
```
def make_inits(n, rng, parent, best_seq, param_seqs):
    inits=[]
    for p in param_seqs:
       if p is not None and p.size:
           inits.append(resample_sequence(p,n))
           inits.append(perturb_sequence(resample_sequence(p,n), rng, 0.2))
    if best_seq is not None:
       inits.append(resample_sequence(best_seq,n)); inits.append(perturb_sequence(...,0.3))
    if parent is not None:
       inits.append(resample_sequence(parent,n)); inits.append(perturb_sequence(...,0.5))
    inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    for alpha in [0.5,1,3]: inits.append(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n,alpha)))
    for _ in range(3): inits.append(smooth_random_init(n,rng))
    inits.append(block_init([1/3,2/3],n)) # two block
    inits.append(block_init([1/3,0,2/3,0],n))
    inits.append(block_init([0.38,0.12,0.38,0.12],n))
    inits.append(block_init([0.12,0.38,0.12,0.38],n))
    inits.append(basis_q_init(n,-0.1,0.8)); basis -0.2,1.2; -0.3,1.6 signs
    # random block
    inits.append(block_init(rng.dirichlet(np.arange(1,5)), n))
    # limit to 16 by unique? Just return first 14 maybe.
    # To avoid duplicates, we can keep all and tasks chunks will handle; but too many.
    # Use random selection? deterministic first.
    return inits[:16]
```

def peak_pair_perturb(a,c,rng,frac):
   n=len(a); k=int(np.argmax(c)); idx=np.arange(n); j=k-idx; mask=(j>=0)&(j<n)
   i_valid=idx[mask]; j_valid=j[mask]
   if len(i_valid)==0: return a
   prod=a[i_valid]*a[j_valid]
   p=int(np.argmax(prod)); i=int(i_valid[p]); j=int(j_valid[p])
   trial=a.copy()
   if trial[i]==0 and trial[j]==0: return trial
   # move from smaller to larger
   if trial[i] < trial[j]:
       src=i; dst=j
   else: src=dst; dst? Let's set src smaller, dst larger.
   delta = min(trial[src], frac*trial[dst]) * 0.5? If move too much, src may zero. Use delta=frac*trial[src] maybe (move up to 100% of smaller). ```

For frac small, might increase. So use frac near 0.5-1.0 to decrease. `frac` choices [0.5,0.8,1.0,1.5] with delta=min(src, frac*src? If frac>1 could zero). Wait delta=frac*trial[src]; if frac=1 zero. Detailed code chunks:

```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=max(1,int(x))
    p=1
    while p<x: p*=2
    return p
```

`soft_weights(c,T)`:
```
xc=T*c
xc_max=np.max(xc)
if not np.isfinite(xc_max): # fallback uniform?
   return np.full(len(c),1/len(c))
if xc_max < 1e-300: return uniform
exp=np.exp(xc-xc_max)
s=exp.sum()
if s<=0: return uniform
return exp/s
```

n=len(a); L=2*n-1
wr=w[::-1]
fa=fft(a,size); fw=fft(wr,size)
conv=np.real(ifft(fa*fw))
# Need first n reversed from L-1
g=2.0*conv[L-1::-1][:n]
g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
return g
```

`param_objective`:
```
def param_objective(theta):
    n=_PARAM_OPT_N
    p=param_raw_sequence(theta,n)
    minp=float(np.min(p))
    pen=0.0
    if minp < 0.01:
       pole=(0.01-minp); pen=1e8*pole*pole
    pp=np.maximum(p,1e-4)
    a=normalize_sequence(pp,n)
    sc=score_internal(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return 1e12+pen
    return sc+pen
```

def run_param_search(deadline, rng):
   cands=[]; best_sc=np.inf
   if minimize is None or deadline <= time.monotonic(): return cands
   starts=[]
   # curated
   for A in (0.1,0.18,0.28,0.4):
     for B in (0.6,0.9,1.2,-0.6,-0.9,-1.2):
       for C in (0.0,0.5,-0.5):
          starts.append([A,B,C,0.0,0.0])
   # random 8
   for _ in range(8): ...
   # Limit: perhaps choose 16 using pseudo every? starts = starts[:16] + random? Let's do starts = starts[:20]
   for n in (64,128):
      for x0 in starts:
         if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
         set_param_opt_n(n)
         x0_arr=np.array(x0,float)
         best_x=x0_arr.copy(); best_f=float(param_objective(x0_arr))
         try:
            res=minimize(param_objective, x0_arr, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter': 80, 'xatol':1e-4, 'fatol':1e-6, 'adaptive': True})
            if hasattr(res,'x') and hasattr(res,'fun'):
                f=float(res.fun)
                if f < best_f:
                   best_f=f; best_x=np.array(res.x,float)
         except Exception:
            pass
         if best_f < best_sc*1.05? and finite:
             p=param_raw_sequence(best_x,n); p=np.maximum(p,1e-4); seq=normalize_sequence(p,n); sc=score_internal(seq)
             if finite and sc < 2.1:
                cands.append((sc,seq))
   cands.sort(key=pool_sort_helper)
   return [seq for sc,seq in cands[:10]]

def try_try_lp_update(a,M,deadline):
   n=len(a); if ...
   # build A once
   base=np.arange(n)
   rows = np.tile(base, n) + np.repeat(base, n)
   cols = np.repeat(base, n)
   data = np.tile(a, n)
   A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
   obj=-np.ones(n)
   best_trial=None; best_sc=score_internal(a)
   for factor in (1.0,1.1,1.25,1.5,2.0):
      tl=max(1.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-2.0)
      try:
         res=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M*factor), bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit':tl})
      except TypeError:
         res=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=..., bounds=..., method='highs')
      except Exception:
         res=None
      if res is not None and getattr(res,'status',1)==0:
         b=np.asarray(res.x,float); B=float(np.sum(b))
         if B > 1.0 + 1e-5:
            g=b/B
            for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75,1.0):
               trial=(1.0-t)*a + t*g
               trial=normalize_sequence(trial,n)
               c=fft_convolution(trial,next_pow2(2*n)); sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
               if sc < best_sc:
                  best_sc=sc; best_trial=trial.copy()
            if best_trial is not None: return (best_trial,best_sc)
   return None
```

```
def peak_pair_perturb(a,c,rng,frac):
    n=a.size
    if n<2: return a.copy()
    k=int(np.argmax(c))
    idx=np.arange(n)
    j=k-idx
    mask=(j>=0)&(j<n)
    if not np.any(mask): return a.copy()
    i_valid=idx[mask]
    j_valid=j[mask]
    prod=a[i_valid]*a[j_valid]
    p=int(np.argmax(prod))
    i=int(i_valid[p]); j=int(j_valid[p])
    trial=a.copy()
    x=trial[i]; y=trial[j]
    if x <= 0.0 and y <= 0.0:
        return trial
    if x < y:
       src=i; dst=j
    else:
       src=j; dst=i
    if trial[src] <=0:
       return trial
    delta = float(min(trial[src], frac * trial[src]))
    # if frac >1, delta=src
    if frac > 1.0:
       delta = trial[src]
    else:
       delta = frac * trial[src]
    trial[src] = max(0.0, trial[src]-delta)
    trial[dst] += delta
    return normalize_sequence(trial,n)
```
If frac=1.5, delta=src -> zero smaller, product 0. Good.
If x,y close, moving smaller to larger may initially increase if delta small? For frac=0.5 and x≈y, new product: x=1,y=0.9,δ=0.45 => x=1.45,y=0.45 product .6525 <0.9, good. If x=2,y=1, δ=0.5 => 2.5*0.5=1.25 <2. Good. So frac 0.5 works mostly.

`perturb_sequence`:
```
def perturb_sequence(seq, rng, sigma):
    n=seq.size
    a=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,1e-12,None)
    loga=np.log(a)
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,3))
    if mode==0:
       loga += sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif mode==1:
       width=int(rng.integers(max(2, n//10), max(3, n//2)))
       start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-width)))
       stop=min(n,start+width)
       loga[start:stop] += sigma * rng.standard_normal(stop-start)
    else:
       # split first half vs second
       loga[:n//2] += sigma * rng.standard_normal(n//2)
       loga[n//2:] -= sigma * rng.standard_normal(n - n//2)
    loga=np.clip(loga, -30.0, 30.0)
    b=np.exp(loga)
    b[np.where(b < 1e-12)] = 0.0
    return normalize_sequence(b,n)
```
If n//2 zero for n=1, but lengths >=8.

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(arr,n):
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float)
    if a.ndim !=1: a=a.reshape(-1)
    if a.size != n:
       # if wrong, resample? Just ones
       if a.size>0:
          a=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,a.size), a)
       else: a=np.ones(n)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
       return np.full(n, 1.0/float(n))
    return a/s
```

`resample_sequence` uses normalize.

`make_inits`: Some inits may be identical; no issue.

Let's write local code:
```
def local_optimize(seq, rng, deadline, do_lp=False, lp_interval=100, max_lp=10):
    a=normalize_sequence(seq, len(seq))
    n=a.shape[0]
    if n<2:
       return a.copy(), 2.0
    csize=next_pow2(2*n); gsize=next_pow2(4*n)
    c=fft_convolution(a,csize)
    cur=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
    best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
    alpha=1.0; T=float(max(10.0, 20.0*n)); no=0; step=0; lp_calls=0; last_lp=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c=fft_convolution(a,csize)
        cur=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
        if cur < best_score:
           best_score=cur; best=a.copy(); no=0
        w=soft_weights(c,T)
        grad=gradient_from_weights(a,w,gsize)
        gmax=float(np.max(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
           break
        g=grad/gmax
        mean_a=float(np.mean(a))
        best_trial=None; best_trial_score=cur; best_trial_c=c
        for al in (alpha, 0.4*alpha, 2.5*alpha):
            if al <=0 or not np.isfinite(al): continue
            trial=a - al*mean_a*g
            trial=normalize_sequence(trial,n)
            tc=fft_convolution(trial,csize)
            ts=2.0*n*float(np.max(tc))
            if ts < best_trial_score:
               best_trial_score=ts; best_trial=trial; best_trial_c=tc
        if best_trial is not None and best_trial_score < cur:
            a=best_trial; c=best_trial_c; cur=best_trial_score
            alpha=min(6.0, alpha*1.15); no=0
        else:
            no +=1
            alpha=max(0.05, alpha*0.85)
            if no > 35:
                if np.random? use rng
                if rng.random() < 0.4:
                    c_best=fft_convolution(best,csize)
                    frac=float(rng.choice([0.5,0.8,1.0,1.2]))
                    a=peak_pair_perturb(best,c_best,rng,frac)
                else:
                    sigma=float(rng.choice([0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]))
                    a=perturb_sequence(best,rng,sigma)
                c=fft_convolution(a,csize); cur=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
                if cur < best_score: best...
                alpha=1.0; no=0
        T=min(80.0*n, T*1.01)
        step +=1
        if do_lp and lp_calls < max_lp and step - last_lp >= lp_interval and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
            M=best_score/(2.0*n)
            res=try_lp_update(best,M,deadline)
            lp_calls +=1; last_lp=step
            if res is not None:
               nb,nsc=res
               if nsc < best_score:
                  best=nb.copy(); best_score=nsc
                  a=best.copy()
                  c=fft_convolution(a,csize); cur=best_score
                  no=0; alpha=1.0
    c=fft_convolution(a,csize); cur=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
    if cur < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
    return best.copy(), float(best_score)
```
Potential issue: If `a` becomes all zeros due subtract? normalize returns uniform. Good.

```
def try_lp_update(a, M, deadline):
    if linprog is None or csr_matrix is None: return None
    n=a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 1500: return None  # 1500? maybe 1024. We have 512 max. Use 2048.
    if M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M): return None
    base=np.arange(n)
    rows = np.tile(base, n) + np.repeat(base, n)
    cols = np.repeat(base, n)
    data = np.tile(np.asarray(a,float), n)
    try:
       A = csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
    except Exception:
       return None
    obj = -np.ones(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    best_trial=None; best_sc=score_internal(a)
    for factor in (1.0, 1.2, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0: break
       tl = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
       try:
          res=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M*factor), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit':tl})
       except Exception:
          try:
             res=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M*factor), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
          except Exception:
             res=None
       if res is not None and getattr(res,'status',1)==0:
          b=np.asarray(res.x,float); B=float(np.sum(b))
          if B > 1.0 + 1e-5:
             g=b/B
             csize=next_pow2(2*n)
             for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75,1.0):
                trial=(1.0-t)*a + t*g
                trial=normalize_sequence(trial,n)
                c=fft_convolution(trial,csize)
                sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc=sc; best_trial=trial.copy()
             if best_trial is not None:
                return (best_trial, float(best_sc))
    return None
```

```python
"""
Search nonnegative step-function coefficients minimizing:
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)**2)

The program uses a hybrid search:
- low-dimensional smooth Fourier/B-spline-like parameter optimization,
- random / block / Fourier initializations,
- custom projected gradient / multiplicative local search with FFT gradients,
- optional LP directions (cross-convolution best response) when time allows.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
except Exception:
    csr_matrix = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


_PARAM_OPT_N = 64


def next_pow2(x):
    x = max(1, int(x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p *= 2
    return p


def pool_sort_helper(item):
    return item[0]


def normalize_sequence(arr, n):
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    if a.size != n:
        if a.size > 0:
            a = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n),
                          np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size),
                          a)
        else:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n))
    return a / s


def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=float)
    if seq.size <= 0:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    val = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.clip(seq, 0.0, None))
    return normalize_sequence(val, n)


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        arr = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if arr is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float)
        if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
            return None
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s < 1e-12:
            return None
        return arr / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def fft_convolution(a, size=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if size is None:
        size = next_pow2(2 * n)
    fa = fft(a, size)
    c = np.real(ifft(fa * fa))[:2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def score_internal(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c) / (s * s))


def soft_weights(c, T):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c.copy()
    xc = float(T) * c
    mx = float(np.max(xc))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size)
    e = np.exp(xc - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size)
    return e / s


def gradient_from_weights(a, w, size):
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    wr = w[::-1]
    fa = fft(a, size)
    fw = fft(wr, size)
    conv = np.real(ifft(fa * fw))
    g = 2.0 * conv[L - 1::-1][:n]
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)


def perturb_sequence(seq, rng, sigma):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return a.copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)
    loga = np.log(a)
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
    if mode == 0:
        loga += float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif mode == 1:
        lo = max(2, n // 10)
        hi = max(3, n // 2)
        width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        stop = min(n, start + width)
        loga[start:stop] += float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(stop - start)
    else:
        h = max(1, n // 2)
        loga[:h] += float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(h)
        loga[h:] -= float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(n - h)
    loga = np.clip(loga, -30.0, 30.0)
    b = np.exp(loga)
    b = np.where(b > 1e-12, b, 0.0)
    return normalize_sequence(b, n)


def peak_pair_perturb(a, c, rng, frac):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a.copy()
    k = int(np.argmax(c))
    idx = np.arange(n)
    j = k - idx
    mask = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
    if not np.any(mask):
        return a.copy()
    i_valid = idx[mask]
    j_valid = j[mask]
    prod = a[i_valid] * a[j_valid]
    p = int(np.argmax(prod))
    i = int(i_valid[p])
    j = int(j_valid[p])
    trial = a.copy()
    x = trial[i]
    y = trial[j]
    if x <= 0.0 and y <= 0.0:
        return trial
    if x < y:
        src, dst = i, j
    else:
        src, dst = j, i
    if trial[src] <= 0.0:
        return trial
    frac = max(0.0, float(frac))
    delta = min(trial[src], frac * trial[src])
    trial[src] = max(0.0, trial[src] - delta)
    trial[dst] += delta
    return normalize_sequence(trial, n)


def block_init(weights, n):
    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.clip(w, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    w = w / s
    K = w.size
    sizes = np.full(K, n // K, dtype=int)
    rem = n - sizes.sum()
    if rem > 0:
        sizes[:rem] += 1
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    pos = 0
    for wi, sz in zip(w, sizes):
        if sz > 0:
            out[pos:pos + sz] = wi / float(sz)
        pos += sz
    return normalize_sequence(out, n)


def smooth_random_init(n, rng, K=4):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.5))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase)
    return normalize_sequence(p, n)


def basis_q_init(n, c, d):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    p = 1.0 + float(c) * (3.0 * y * y - 1.0) + float(d) * y * (1.0 - y * y)
    return normalize_sequence(np.maximum(p, 1e-3), n)


def make_inits(n, rng, parent, best_seq, param_seqs):
    inits = []

    for p in param_seqs:
        if p is None or np.asarray(p).size == 0:
            continue
        base = resample_sequence(p, n)
        inits.append(base)
        inits.append(perturb_sequence(base, rng, 0.15))

    if best_seq is not None:
        base = resample_sequence(best_seq, n)
        inits.append(base)
        inits.append(perturb_sequence(base, rng, 0.25))

    if parent is not None:
        base = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        inits.append(base)
        inits.append(perturb_sequence(base, rng, 0.45))

    inits.append(np.ones(n) / float(n))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.5):
        inits.append(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha)))

    for _ in range(3):
        inits.append(smooth_random_init(n, rng, K=4))
    for _ in range(2):
        inits.append(smooth_random_init(n, rng, K=6))

    inits.append(block_init([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], n))
    inits.append(block_init([1.0 / 3.0, 0.0, 2.0 / 3.0, 0.0], n))
    inits.append(block_init([0.38, 0.12, 0.38, 0.12], n))
    inits.append(block_init([0.12, 0.38, 0.12, 0.38], n))
    inits.append(block_init([0.7, 0.3, 0.7, 0.3, 0.7, 0.3, 0.7, 0.3], n))
    inits.append(block_init([0.7, 0.7, 0.3, 0.3, 0.7, 0.7, 0.3, 0.3], n))
    inits.append(block_init([0.20, 0.05, 0.25, 0.10, 0.20, 0.05, 0.25, 0.10], n))
    inits.append(block_init(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(6)), n))
    inits.append(block_init(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(8)), n))
    inits.append(block_init(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(12)), n))

    inits.append(basis_q_init(n, -0.10, 0.80))
    inits.append(basis_q_init(n, -0.10, -0.80))
    inits.append(basis_q_init(n, -0.20, 1.20))
    inits.append(basis_q_init(n, -0.20, -1.20))
    inits.append(basis_q_init(n, -0.30, 1.60))
    inits.append(basis_q_init(n, -0.30, -1.60))

    return inits[:22]


def set_param_opt_n(n):
    global _PARAM_OPT_N
    _PARAM_OPT_N = max(8, int(n))


def param_raw_sequence(theta, n):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    A = float(theta[0]) if len(theta) > 0 else 0.0
    B = float(theta[1]) if len(theta) > 1 else 0.0
    C = float(theta[2]) if len(theta) > 2 else 0.0
    D = float(theta[3]) if len(theta) > 3 else 0.0
    E = float(theta[4]) if len(theta) > 4 else 0.0

    p = 1.0
    p += A * (4.0 * x * (1.0 - x) - 1.0)
    p += B * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    p += C * (y * (1.0 - y * y))
    p += D * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    p += E * (3.0 * y * y - 1.0)
    return p


def param_objective(theta):
    n = int(_PARAM_OPT_N)
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    p = param_raw_sequence(theta, n)
    minp = float(np.min(p))
    pen = 0.0
    if minp < 0.01:
        pole = 0.01 - minp
        pen = 1e8 * pole * pole
    pp = np.maximum(p, 1e-4)
    a = normalize_sequence(pp, n)
    sc = score_internal(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e12 + pen
    return sc + pen


def run_param_search(deadline, rng):
    cands = []
    if minimize is None:
        return cands
    if deadline <= time.monotonic():
        return cands

    curated = []
    for A in (0.12, 0.20, 0.30, 0.45):
        for B in (0.6, -0.6, 0.9, -0.9):
            for C in (0.3, -0.3, 0.0):
                curated.append([A, B, C, 0.0, 0.0])
    starts = curated[:16]

    for _ in range(6):
        starts.append([
            float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.55)),
            float(rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.2)),
            float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)),
            float(rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8)),
            float(rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)),
        ])

    best_sc = np.inf

    for n in (64, 128):
        for x0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            set_param_opt_n(n)
            x0a = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).reshape(-1)

            try:
                f0 = float(param_objective(x0a))
            except Exception:
                f0 = np.inf
            best_x = x0a.copy()
            best_f = f0

            try:
                res = minimize(
                    param_objective,
                    x0a,
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxiter": 80, "xatol": 1e-4, "fatol": 1e-6}
                )
                if hasattr(res, "x") and hasattr(res, "fun"):
                    f1 = float(res.fun)
                    if np.isfinite(f1) and f1 < best_f:
                        best_f = f1
                        best_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
            except Exception:
                pass

            if not np.isfinite(best_f):
                continue
            try:
                p = param_raw_sequence(best_x, n)
                p = np.maximum(p, 1e-4)
                seq = normalize_sequence(p, n)
                sc = score_internal(seq)
            except Exception:
                continue

            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 2.1:
                cands.append((float(sc), seq.copy()))

    if len(cands) > 0:
        cands.sort(key=pool_sort_helper)
    return [seq for _sc, seq in cands[:10]]


def add_to_pool(pool, score, seq):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    pool.append((float(score), np.asarray(seq, dtype=float).copy()))
    if len(pool) > 25:
        pool.sort(key=pool_sort_helper)
        del pool[25:]


def try_lp_update(a, M, deadline):
    if linprog is None or csr_matrix is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 1500:
        return None
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    try:
        base = np.arange(n)
        rows = np.tile(base, n) + np.repeat(base, n)
        cols = np.repeat(base, n)
        data = np.tile(a, n)
        A = csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))
    except Exception:
        return None

    obj = -np.ones(n)
    best_trial = None
    best_sc = score_internal(a)
    csize = next_pow2(2 * n)

    for factor in (1.0, 1.2, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * factor)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
        res = None
        try:
            tl = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
            res = linprog(
                obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": tl}
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    obj,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="highs"
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

        if res is None:
            continue
        if getattr(res, "status", 1) != 0:
            continue

        try:
            b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        except Exception:
            continue

        B = float(np.sum(b))
        if B <= 1.0 + 1e-5:
            continue

        g = b / B
        for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            trial = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            trial = normalize_sequence(trial, n)
            c = fft_convolution(trial, csize)
            sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_trial = trial.copy()
        if best_trial is not None:
            return best_trial, float(best_sc)

    return None


def local_optimize(seq, rng, deadline, do_lp=False, lp_interval=100, max_lp=10):
    a = normalize_sequence(seq, len(seq))
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a.copy(), 2.0

    csize = next_pow2(2 * n)
    gsize = next_pow2(4 * n)
    c = fft_convolution(a, csize)
    cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = cur
    alpha = 1.0
    T = float(max(10.0, 20.0 * n))
    no_improve = 0
    step = 0
    lp_calls = 0
    last_lp = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = fft_convolution(a, csize)
        cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
        if cur < best_score:
            best_score = cur
            best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        w = soft_weights(c, T)
        grad = gradient_from_weights(a, w, gsize)
        gmax = float(np.max(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        g = grad / gmax
        mean_a = float(np.mean(a))

        best_trial = None
        best_trial_score = cur
        best_trial_c = c

        for al in (alpha, 0.4 * alpha, 2.5 * alpha):
            if al <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(al):
                continue
            trial = a - al * mean_a * g
            trial = normalize_sequence(trial, n)
            tc = fft_convolution(trial, csize)
            ts = float(2.0 * n * np.max(tc))
            if ts < best_trial_score:
                best_trial_score = ts
                best_trial = trial
                best_trial_c = tc

        if best_trial is not None and best_trial_score < cur:
            a = best_trial
            c = best_trial_c
            cur = best_trial_score
            alpha = min(6.0, alpha * 1.15)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            alpha = max(0.05, alpha * 0.85)
            if no_improve > 35:
                if rng.random() < 0.45:
                    cb = fft_convolution(best, csize)
                    frac = float(rng.choice((0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.2)))
                    a = peak_pair_perturb(best, cb, rng, frac)
                else:
                    sigma = float(rng.choice((0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)))
                    a = perturb_sequence(best, rng, sigma)
                c = fft_convolution(a, csize)
                cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best = a.copy()
                alpha = 1.0
                no_improve = 0

        T = min(80.0 * n, T * 1.01)
        step += 1

        if (do_lp and lp_calls < max_lp and
                step - last_lp >= lp_interval and
                time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0):
            M = best_score / (2.0 * n)
            res = try_lp_update(best, M, deadline)
            lp_calls += 1
            last_lp = step
            if res is not None:
                nb, nsc = res
                if nsc < best_score:
                    best = nb.copy()
                    best_score = nsc
                    a = best.copy()
                    c = fft_convolution(a, csize)
                    cur = best_score
                    no_improve = 0
                    alpha = 1.0

    c = fft_convolution(a, csize)
    cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    if cur < best_score:
        best = a.copy()
        best_score = cur
    return best.copy(), float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    """Hybrid search over nonnegative sequences. Returns best list found."""
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 1500.0
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, budget - 20.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = get_parent_sequence()

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf
    pool = []

    print(f"[init] budget={budget:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # Phase 1: low-dimensional smooth design search.
    param_budget = start_time + min(90.0, 0.15 * budget)
    if param_budget > time.monotonic() + 1.0:
        try:
            param_seqs = run_param_search(param_budget, rng)
        except Exception:
            param_seqs = []
        if param_seqs:
            print(f"[param] best param score={score_internal(param_seqs[0]):.6f}", flush=True)
    else:
        param_seqs = []

    for p in param_seqs:
        if p is None:
            continue
        sc = score_internal(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_score:
                best_seq = p.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
                add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best_seq)
            else:
                add_to_pool(pool, float(sc), p)

    if budget < 300.0:
        lengths = [16, 32, 64, 128]
    elif budget < 700.0:
        lengths = [8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256]
    else:
        lengths = [8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512]

    # Phase 2: multi-start local search.
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        inits = make_inits(n, rng, parent, best_seq, param_seqs)
        if not inits:
            continue

        for idx, init in enumerate(inits):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

            if budget < 300.0:
                chunk = 3.0
            elif n <= 64:
                chunk = 8.0
            elif n <= 256:
                chunk = 6.0
            else:
                chunk = 5.0

            if best_score < 1.5027:
                chunk = min(chunk, 4.0)

            end = time.monotonic() + min(chunk, max(0.5, deadline - time.monotonic()))

            do_lp = n <= 128
            if n <= 32:
                lp_interval, max_lp = 40, 6
            elif n <= 64:
                lp_interval, max_lp = 80, 4
            elif n <= 128:
                lp_interval, max_lp = 120, 3
            else:
                lp_interval, max_lp = 200, 2

            seq, sc = local_optimize(init, rng, end, do_lp=do_lp,
                                     lp_interval=lp_interval, max_lp=max_lp)

            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue

            if sc < best_score:
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
                add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best_seq)
                if best_score < 1.9:
                    param_seqs.append(best_seq.copy())
                    if len(param_seqs) > 14:
                        param_seqs = param_seqs[:14]
                print(f"[search] n={n} init={idx} score={sc:.6f} "
                      f"best={best_score:.6f} t={time.monotonic()-start_time:.1f}",
                      flush=True)
            else:
                if sc <= best_score + 0.25:
                    add_to_pool(pool, float(sc), seq)

    if best_seq is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = np.full(64, 1.0 / 64.0)
        best_score = 2.0

    # Phase 3: polish promising candidates.
    if pool:
        pool.sort(key=pool_sort_helper)
        top = []
        for _sc, _seq in pool:
            if len(top) >= 5:
                break
            if _sc <= best_score + 0.25:
                top.append(_seq.copy())

        if top:
            pool_deadline = min(
                deadline,
                time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.25 * max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
            )
            rem = len(top)
            for seq in top:
                if time.monotonic() >= pool_deadline:
                    break
                rem -= 1
                slice_time = 60.0
                if rem > 1:
                    slice_time = min(slice_time, max(5.0, (pool_deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(rem + 1)))
                end = time.monotonic() + min(slice_time, max(0.5, pool_deadline - time.monotonic()))
                seq2, sc2 = local_optimize(seq, rng, end, do_lp=True, lp_interval=180, max_lp=25)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_seq = seq2.copy()
                    best_score = float(sc2)
                    add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best_seq)
                    print(f"[polish] score={sc2:.6f} len={len(best_seq)} "
                          f"t={time.monotonic()-start_time:.1f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 4: final deep polish on the current global best.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        seq2, sc2 = local_optimize(best_seq, rng, deadline, do_lp=True,
                                   lp_interval=300, max_lp=80)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
            best_seq = seq2.copy()
            best_score = float(sc2)

    if best_seq is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = np.full(64, 1.0 / 64.0)
        best_score = 2.0

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=float).reshape(-1)]
    if not out or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [1.0] * 64

    try:
        actual = evaluate_sequence(out)
        if np.isfinite(actual):
            print(f"[final] internal={best_score:.6f} actual={float(actual):.6f} len={len(out)}",
                  flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"[final] internal={best_score:.6f} actual=inf, using fallback", flush=True)
            out = [1.0] * 64
    except Exception:
        print(f"[final] internal={best_score:.6f} eval unavailable", flush=True)

    return out
```
